# CloudTCP schema capability gaps

This appendix gives the complete capability-to-clause mapping used by the audit. Read it with [report.md](report.md) and the [clause matrix](rule-gap-matrix.csv).

Counts are overlapping static source/schema mappings of candidate related checks. They are not guaranteed unlock counts, actual findings, or a compliance percentage. A permission can need additional context when selected without requiring that treatment on every plan. Medium-confidence mappings require clause-specific review before choosing an exact future field contract.

G37 is a cross-cutting evidence-completeness concern, not an extra requirement attributed to a specific source clause. Proposed additions describe capabilities; property names in rejected probes are illustrative.

## G01 road classification

**Required facts:** Roadway functional/environment classification, divided/access-controlled status, and State-highway ownership

**Current limitation:** site contains names and state but no functional road class, urban/rural context, access-control, divided-road status, or highway ownership.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add explicit roadway classification, environment, access-control and ownership; do not infer these from road_name, highway_number, city or speed.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/site`, `/properties/typical_application`.

**Candidate impact:** 62 distinct canonical checks, 68 source clauses; 19 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 1 foundation.

**Full document clauses:** [Z-019.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP%20Rules%20Document.md#L109), **Additional clause-specific facts:** road category and agency speed class; approach and warning endpoints; assigned signs and encounter order; single-sign low-speed residential applicability; advance-warning extent; mount kind and bottom height; pavement/curb/sidewalk datum; context and secondary-sign parent; secondary-sign relation; applicable parent height/datum; roadside category and curb; pavement/curb face. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- Actual dimensions and light/road context needed; space judgment belongs to c02.
- Authoritative table/approximation and first-sign policy required; supply missing optional facing.
- Barrier is represented; purpose/associations are missing.
- Centerline offset cannot establish edge/curb offset.
- Centerline offset is horizontal, not height.

## G02 approaches and sign associations

**Required facts:** Approach identity, travel orientation, restriction references and sign/closure associations

**Current limitation:** direction is one site-level cardinal value; zones are station intervals; no approach objects or sign-to-approach/closure references.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add identified directed approaches, restriction references and explicit sign-to-approach/closure links.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/typical_application/properties/direction`, `/properties/zones`, `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/0/properties/zone_id`.

**Candidate impact:** 94 distinct canonical checks, 104 source clauses; 11 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 1 foundation.

**Full document clauses:** [Z-007.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP%20Rules%20Document.md#L37), **Representative focused probes:** `warning_sequence_ordered`, `warning_sequence_reversed`, `warning_sequence_missing_facing`, `warning_sequence_unknown_code`, `warning_sequence_tied_station`, `sign_side_explicit_facing`. See [focused-results.json](evidence/probes/focused-results.json).

**Additional clause-specific facts:** road category and agency speed class; approach and warning endpoints; assigned signs and encounter order; single-sign low-speed residential applicability; advance-warning extent; explicit facing; unique warning/transition approach; supported warning code; encounter order; crossroads/major driveways entering zone; access volume significance; assigned warning signs. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- A road_name string does not identify which roadway each sign serves.
- AFAD equipment and human operator require distinct identity/relationships.
- As-needed placement requires scoped judgment.
- Authoritative table/approximation and first-sign policy required; supply missing optional facing.
- BOTH does not establish alternating use of the same lane.

## G03 lane identity and flow

**Required facts:** Directional lane IDs, original/receiving/closed lanes and alternating-flow arrangements

**Current limitation:** lanes_total/lanes_closed are counts; direction=BOTH is not an alternating-flow or divided-roadway declaration.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add lane inventory with travel direction, original/temporary role, closures, receiving-lane references and alternating-flow intent.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/typical_application/properties/lanes_total`, `/properties/typical_application/properties/lanes_closed`.

**Candidate impact:** 104 distinct canonical checks, 107 source clauses; 26 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 1 foundation.

**Representative focused probes:** `sign_through_lane_applicability_unmodeled`, `arrow_boards_no_lane_identity`, `arrow_shoulder_caution`, `arrow_shoulder_arrow_mode`, `arrow_two_lanes_no_traffic_pattern`. See [focused-results.json](evidence/probes/focused-results.json).

**Additional clause-specific facts:** through lanes; adjacent closed-lane group; left/right closure; assigned advance sign; shared lane serving both directions; constriction start; W20-4 approach; full/local-only closure; closure point; R11-2/Type3 support; shoulder travel-lane use; assigned taper. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- AFAD equipment and human operator require distinct identity/relationships.
- As practicable requires judgment; taper endpoints exist, association does not.
- BOTH does not establish alternating use of the same lane.
- Conditional could-lead-traffic-off-path trigger requires judgment.
- Counting boards is insufficient to prove each lane is served.

## G04 device taper lane relationships

**Required facts:** Device-line membership in a taper/tangent/closure and intended traffic-path geometry

**Current limitation:** zone_id links only a station interval; no taper_id, lane_id, traffic-path role, traffic-space geometry or lane-device relationships.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add explicit device-line-to-taper/lane/path relationships and intended traffic-path geometry.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/1`, `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/2/properties/zone_id`, `/properties/zones`.

**Candidate impact:** 156 distinct canonical checks, 161 source clauses; 26 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 1 foundation.

**Additional clause-specific facts:** taper type; assigned device gaps; approved about20ft tolerance; closed-lane/work relation; vehicle/board/CMS upstream edge; taper/tangent relation; actual spacing; device kind including barricades; path misdirection risk; transition end; intended lane/path; legal closure with local access allowed. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- <=S or >2S is decidable; S<spacing<=2S can remain ambiguous.
- Approximate 20-ft spacing has no authored acceptance tolerance, and device-to-taper association is absent.
- As practicable requires judgment; taper endpoints exist, association does not.
- Barrier is represented; purpose/associations are missing.
- Conditional could-lead-traffic-off-path trigger requires judgment.

## G05 shoulder and road edges

**Required facts:** Paved shoulder dimensions, pavement/traveled-way/curb/median edges, occupation, overhang and structural suitability

**Current limitation:** station offsets locate elements relative to a centerline but no road-edge, curb, shoulder-width or load-bearing geometry exists.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add road cross-section/edge/curb and shoulder geometry with paved/strength properties and work/equipment occupation.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/zones`, `/properties/elements`, `#/$defs/station_offset`.

**Candidate impact:** 66 distinct canonical checks, 67 source clauses; 8 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 2 safety geometry.

Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- As practicable requires judgment; taper endpoints exist, association does not.
- Centerline offset cannot establish edge/curb offset.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- External Green Book/Roadside Design Guide design criteria are not reproduced in the repository documents.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.

## G06 operation phases

**Required facts:** Duration, stationary/mobile status, daylight/night operation and closure setup/removal sequence

**Current limitation:** temporal_context is a snapshot; it has no operation start/end, duration, mobility, lighting condition, phase or ordered action history.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add work duration/mobility, explicit daylight/night conditions and phase/action records when a rule concerns installation/removal sequence.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/temporal_context`, `/properties/typical_application`.

**Candidate impact:** 60 distinct canonical checks, 60 source clauses; 17 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 1 foundation.

Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- AFAD equipment and human operator require distinct identity/relationships.
- Actual dimensions and light/road context needed; space judgment belongs to c02.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Device elimination is permitted; hazard substitution is a separate mandatory restriction.
- Equipment/state predicates; mounting practicability belongs to c01.

## G07 work vehicles and occupancy

**Required facts:** Workers, equipment/material/vehicle footprints, active lighting and work/shadow/transfer vehicle roles

**Current limitation:** elements supports only sign, taper, device_line, arrow_board and flagger; no vehicle/work/storage/illumination entities.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add work/storage footprints and work/shadow/transfer vehicle records with operational status, location, lights, attenuators and relevant relationships.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf`.

**Candidate impact:** 46 distinct canonical checks, 48 source clauses; 5 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- A road_name string does not identify which roadway each sign serves.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Device elimination is permitted; hazard substitution is a separate mandatory restriction.
- Engineering judgment remains necessary; fields cannot determine the appropriate adjustment.
- Equipment/state predicates; mounting practicability belongs to c01.

## G08 traffic and assessment

**Required facts:** Approach/lane traffic demands, capacity, heavy-vehicle mix, queues and operational feasibility assessment

**Current limitation:** observations has optional point volume/speed/queue but lacks lane/approach attribution, capacity, vehicle mix and documented engineering assessment; predictions are model output.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add provenance-qualified approach/lane demand, capacity and vehicle-mix inputs plus explicit operational/design assessment records.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/observations`, `/properties/predictions`.

**Candidate impact:** 39 distinct canonical checks, 39 source clauses; 4 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 2 safety geometry.

## G09 lane width and encroachment

**Required facts:** Usable lane width measured to device near-face/pavement edges and work encroachment

**Current limitation:** lane_width_ft belongs to an individual taper and does not define remaining lane geometry, equipment extent or device-face dimensions.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add lane boundaries, clear-width measurement endpoints, work encroachment and relevant device footprints.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/1/properties/lane_width_ft`, `/properties/typical_application`.

**Candidate impact:** 35 distinct canonical checks, 35 source clauses; 1 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 2 safety geometry.

## G10 intersection turns

**Required facts:** Crossroad/driveway/intersection topology, receiving approaches and turning traffic movements

**Current limitation:** intersection_closure is only an application type; there is no intersection, turn-bay, crossroad, driveway, roundabout or movement inventory.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add identified intersections, crossroads/driveways, turn bays and directed turning movements.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/typical_application/properties/type`, `/properties/zones`.

**Candidate impact:** 22 distinct canonical checks, 25 source clauses; 1 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 2 safety geometry.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- A road_name string does not identify which roadway each sign serves.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.

- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.
- Major/high-volume classification requires an authored criterion.

## G11 ramp geometry

**Required facts:** Entrance/exit/temporary ramp identity, access and merging/acceleration/deceleration geometry

**Current limitation:** ramp_closure is a category; ramp connections, acceleration/deceleration lane lengths and merge relationships cannot be encoded.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add ramp and mainline topology, acceleration/deceleration segments, entrance/exit states and reference endpoints.

- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- External Green Book/Roadside Design Guide design criteria are not reproduced in the repository documents.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.
- Retain site-specific engineering/authority determination; adding data fields does not authorize a decision.
- Schema 1.0.0 does not encode horizontal/vertical alignment, curve endpoints, and design criteria; ramp geometry, acceleration/deceleration lengths, and approach association required by Z-113 (TA-42 exit-ramp alignment design dependency).

## G12 alignment

**Required facts:** Horizontal/vertical alignment, curve/crest endpoints, grade and design-speed criteria

**Current limitation:** No centerline coordinates, curve, profile or design-speed records exist; station_offset is a local scalar location.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add reference alignment and profile, curves/crests/grades and selected design criteria.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/site/properties/coordinate_system`, `#/$defs/station_offset`, `/properties/zones`.

## G13 sight and stopping

**Required facts:** Available directional sight distance, intended stop points, sight lines and visibility constraints

**Current limitation:** visibility_mi is weather/context visibility rather than measured approach stopping/passing sight distance; no stopping-point or sight-line geometry.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add measured approach stopping/passing sight distance and stopping/control reference points, with basis and visibility constraints.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/temporal_context/properties/visibility_mi`, `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/4/properties/position`.

**Candidate impact:** 29 distinct canonical checks, 34 source clauses; 3 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 2 safety geometry.

- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Engineering approval cannot be inferred from insufficient sight.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.
- Retain site-specific engineering/authority determination; adding data fields does not authorize a decision.
- Single-flagger permission needs actual geometry/visibility, not count.

## G14 buffer geometry

**Required facts:** Lateral buffer footprint/width, occupancy and geometric role relative to flows, work and control points

**Current limitation:** zones supports only upstream_buffer/downstream_buffer station intervals; no lateral buffer type, width, side, occupancy or lane/flow associations.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add explicit lateral/longitudinal buffer role, footprint, width and relationships to lanes, work and stopping points; retain optionality.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/zones/items/properties/type`, `/properties/zones/items/properties/start_station`, `/properties/zones/items/properties/end_station`.

**Candidate impact:** 52 distinct canonical checks, 52 source clauses; 0 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 2 safety geometry.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.
- Retain site-specific engineering/authority determination; adding data fields does not authorize a decision.
- Schema 1.0.0 does not encode buffer occupancy, lateral width, grade, and intended stopping geometry; taper/device-to-lane associations and intended traffic-path geometry required by T-079 (TA-22 merging taper geometry).
- Schema 1.0.0 does not encode intersection/crossroad/driveway geometry and turning movements; buffer occupancy, lateral width, grade, and intended stopping geometry; taper/device-to-lane associations and intended traffic-path geometry required by T-083 (TA-24 intersection taper geometry).

## G15 barrier properties

**Required facts:** Barrier type, traffic role, crashworthiness, shielding and delineation

**Current limitation:** device=barrier denotes only a device-line category; no temporary/movable subtype, protected-side geometry, crashworthiness or shielding record.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add barrier subtype, safety specifications, shielding/delineation and protected-entity/traffic relationships.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/2/properties/device`.

**Candidate impact:** 24 distinct canonical checks, 24 source clauses; 7 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.
- Retain site-specific engineering/authority determination; adding data fields does not authorize a decision.
- Schema 1.0.0 does not encode bicycle-route geometry and bicycle/vehicle operating conditions; work/shadow-vehicle equipment, activity, location, and relationship to lanes; taper/device-to-lane associations and intended traffic-path geometry required by T-098 (TA-47 bicycle-lane taper).
- Schema 1.0.0 has no numbered typical-application/layout identifier; closure type alone does not establish this source trigger.

## G19 signal control

**Additional clause-specific facts:** major multi-lane; heavy/high-speed/limited sight; stationary/mobile closure; board associations; stationary/mobile lane closure; multi-lane roadway; board mode/closure association; freeway closed lanes; distinct board per lane; shoulder/roadside applicability; two-way versus same-direction lanes; board closure-versus-shift purpose. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- As practicable requires judgment; taper endpoints exist, association does not.
- Counting boards is insufficient to prove each lane is served.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Equipment/state predicates; mounting practicability belongs to c01.
- Explicit shoulder_closure already works; two lanes do not prove two-way.

Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- ADA Sec.307 is referenced, not reproduced; acquire/version the technical requirements.
- ADA external source applies to c02; this clause needs support/facility boundaries.
- Centerline offset cannot establish edge/curb offset.
- Centerline offset is horizontal, not height.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.

## G24 approval and review

**Required facts:** Chosen exception/deviation, responsible authority, approval and documented engineering assessment

**Current limitation:** No approval, engineering-study/judgment, exception/deviation selection or signoff type exists.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add rule-scoped decisions/exceptions/approvals with authority, rationale, applicability, evidence and effective time; retain human judgment.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/typical_application`, `/properties/site`.

**Candidate impact:** 75 distinct canonical checks, 80 source clauses; 4 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- Conditional could-lead-traffic-off-path trigger requires judgment.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Engineering approval cannot be inferred from insufficient sight.
- Engineering judgment remains necessary; fields cannot determine the appropriate adjustment.
- External Green Book/Roadside Design Guide design criteria are not reproduced in the repository documents.

## G25 device specifications

**Required facts:** Individual device dimensions/material/retroreflectivity/light specifications and device groups

**Current limitation:** Device lines specify category and spacing; no individual-device dimensions, physical specs, retroreflectivity or explicit transverse device groups/counts.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add individual devices/groups and dimensions, physical/light/reflective specs where required; distinguish a device footprint from its center.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/2/properties/device`, `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/2/properties/spacing_ft`.

**Candidate impact:** 45 distinct canonical checks, 45 source clauses; 19 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- <=S or >2S is decidable; S<spacing<=2S can remain ambiguous.
- AFAD equipment and human operator require distinct identity/relationships.
- Actual dimensions and light/road context needed; space judgment belongs to c02.
- Barrier is represented; purpose/associations are missing.
- Crashworthy barrier and lane separator are distinct kinds.

## G26 rail crossing queues

**Required facts:** Track crossing extent, actual queue footprint and crossing-specific controller

**Current limitation:** Observed queue_length_ft is available, but tracks, queue-to-track geometry and crossing-specific control roles are absent.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add grade-crossing geometry, actual queue extent/attribution and crossing officer/flagger duties.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/observations/items/properties/queue_length_ft`, `/properties/predictions`.

**Candidate impact:** 4 distinct canonical checks, 4 source clauses; 2 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

**Full document clauses:** [Z-047.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP%20Rules%20Document.md#L330), [Z-047-B.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP%20Rules%20Document.md#L336), [T-074.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP%20Rules%20Document.md#L1560), [B-024.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP%20Rules%20Document.md#L2035).

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.

## G27 barricade types

**Required facts:** Barricade type and rail/group geometry

**Current limitation:** device enum excludes barricades, including Type II/III; barrier is a different device.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add barricade types and rail/group geometry with permitted-use context.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/2/properties/device`.

**Candidate impact:** 11 distinct canonical checks, 13 source clauses; 3 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

**Additional clause-specific facts:** barricade type/rail areas; covered area; sign area; breakaway posts; clearance; full/local-only closure; closure point; R11-2/Type3 support; taper/tangent relation; actual spacing; device kind including barricades; barricade type. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- <=S or >2S is decidable; S<spacing<=2S can remain ambiguous.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.
- Optional size_inches can supply sign dimensions but not rail coverage.
- Type1 permission is optional; permitted-use signage is a separate STANDARD.

## G28 afad equipment

**Required facts:** AFAD identity/equipment, traffic stopping point and attendance/staffing

**Current limitation:** No AFAD device type or attendance/staffing/stop-point relationship exists.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add AFAD device and operating/attendance/staffing records linked to controlled approaches and stopping points.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf`.

**Candidate impact:** 4 distinct canonical checks, 4 source clauses; 3 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

**Full document clauses:** [T-023.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP%20Rules%20Document.md#L1086).

**W1 clauses:** [FLAG-01.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP-W1-1%20Rules.md#L399), [FLAG-08.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP-W1-1%20Rules.md#L441), [FLAG-09.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP-W1-1%20Rules.md#L447).

**Additional clause-specific facts:** actual control device; shared lane; emergency/LEO exception role; AFAD; single approach lane; stop point/taper; method1/2; attended/view; night; duration; AFAD shape/material; bottom height. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- AFAD equipment and human operator require distinct identity/relationships.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.
- controls is direction, not hardware; paddle preference is guidance.

## G29 application layout identity

**Required facts:** Numbered typical application/figure/Standard Plan and specific variant/phase selection

**Current limitation:** typical_application.type is a coarse closure category; numbered TA, figure, jurisdictional variant and Standard Plan are absent.

- External Green Book/Roadside Design Guide design criteria are not reproduced in the repository documents.
- Fig6P29 geometry referenced, not fully reproduced; adopt authoritative requirements.

## G30 speed basis and provenance

**Required facts:** Selected calculation speed and provenance: posted, pre-work off-peak 85th-percentile, anticipated operating or bicycle speed

**Current limitation:** posted_speed_mph/work_zone_speed_mph cannot identify pre-work percentile or anticipated-operating basis; observation speed is an unqualified point reading, with no selected-rule speed basis.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add speed value with basis, vehicle mode, pre-work/measurement time and applicability/selection provenance; do not replace posted speed silently.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/typical_application/properties/posted_speed_mph`, `/properties/typical_application/properties/work_zone_speed_mph`, `/properties/observations/items/properties/speed_mph`.

## G31 sign design and attachments

**Current limitation:** Signs have code, legend and optional size_inches strings but no symbol mirror/orientation or parent/plaque attachment and mounting context.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add sign symbol/attachment relationships and structured dimensions/context where required.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/0/properties/mutcd_code`, `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/0/properties/size_inches`, `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/0/properties/legend`.

## G33 taper kind

**Required facts:** Explicit one-lane two-way taper identity, linked to the controlled path

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- AFAD equipment and human operator require distinct identity/relationships.
- BOTH or downstream labels do not encode this identity.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Full registry uses an explicit not_evaluable placeholder; an evaluator must be authored after facts and source policies are established.
- No source numeric tolerance; geometry alone cannot supply one.

## G34 ttc extent

**Required facts:** Authoritative complete TTC-zone extent, mileage-sign scope and rounding basis

**Current limitation:** Zone intervals do not declare complete project/TTC limits or associate a G20-1 inscription with those limits.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add authoritative TTC boundaries and mileage-sign/approach associations; preserve units and authorized rounding policy.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/zones`, `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/0/properties/legend`.

**Candidate impact:** 2 distinct canonical checks, 2 source clauses; 1 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

**W1 clauses:** [SIGN-26.c01](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP-W1-1%20Rules.md#L153), [SIGN-26.c03](https://github.com/Issacboy123/cloudtcp-python-verifier/blob/ef5e47459aea9559ae8fad7f0087f376308fe09e/TCP-W1-1%20Rules.md#L153).

**Additional clause-specific facts:** authoritative TTC extent; associated G20-1; inscription distance; advance placement. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.
- Stations compute a declared extent; max-minus-min does not prove inventory completeness.

## G35 parking restrictions

**Required facts:** No-parking intervals associated with a closed midblock crossing, curb and approach

**Current limitation:** Signs can carry parking legends, but there is no parking-restriction extent or crossing/curb association.

**Minimum capability addition:** Add parking-restriction extents linked to crosswalk closure, curb geometry and served approaches.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements/items/oneOf/0`, `/properties/zones`.

**Candidate impact:** 1 distinct canonical checks, 1 source clauses; 1 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 3 equipment and specialist evidence.

**Additional clause-specific facts:** midblock crosswalk closure; curb; no-parking extent and approach. Exact facts for every member remain in the matrix.

**Residual blockers, representative examples:**

- 50-ft distance becomes computable after reference/approach is established.
- Default registry has no rule-specific evaluator for this canonical check; adding schema fields alone will not execute it.

**Required facts:** Audible accessibility equipment and its crossing/closure association

## G37 inventory completeness

**Required facts:** Evidence that the relevant drawing/site/device inventory was examined completely

**Current limitation:** elements may be empty; the contract has no scoped inventory-completeness or extraction-coverage declaration. Absence can mean no subject or missing evidence.

**Minimum capability addition:** Define a scoped completeness/evidence contract or accompanying trusted ingestion report before treating absent subjects as confirmed absent. Do not impose a universal minimum device count.

**Current schema locations:** `/properties/elements`, `/properties/site/properties/source_file`.

**Candidate impact:** 0 distinct canonical checks, 0 source clauses; 0 distinct checks have at least one STANDARD clause in this mapping. Suggested group: 1 foundation.

**Evidence:** the synthetic buffer inputs have no elements; absence-based checks can return not_applicable. See [inventory-completeness.json](evidence/inventory-completeness.json). The extractor's separate diagnostics already preserve unmapped/skipped content; propagate that evidence rather than inventing real-world absence.
